No version pinned here deliberately — the published package's own .claude-plugin/plugin.json version is authoritative and takes priority in Claude Code's resolution order, so pinning a second copy here would just be one more place for the two to silently drift apart. - **php-lspx**: PHP language server: intelephense, phpactor, phpantom-lsp, and phpforge fanned out via the bundled lspfan multiplexer for completion, diagnostics, and refactoring ([MarjovanLier/claude-plugins](https://github.com/MarjovanLier/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/php-lspx))
- **php-mcp-development**: Comprehensive resources for building Model Context Protocol servers using the official PHP SDK with attribute-based discovery, including best practices, project generation, and expert assistance ([paulasilvatech/awesome-harness-primitives](https://github.com/paulasilvatech/awesome-harness-primitives/tree/HEAD/harness/claude-code/plugins/php-mcp-development))
- **php-memory-audit**: Audits a Symfony/Doctrine or Laravel/Eloquent codebase for the patterns that cause memory exhaustion and OOM crashes: full entity hydration, identity maps never cleared in loops, N+1 lazy loads, unescaped LIKE wildcards, uncached per-request queries, flush() inside post-events, and whole files buffered into strings. Produces a prioritized findings report, not edits. ([boehpyk/boehpyk-skills](https://github.com/boehpyk/boehpyk-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/php-memory-audit))
- **php-modernization**: PHP 8.x modernization patterns for upgrading legacy PHP applications. Covers constructor property promotion, readonly classes, enums, attributes, generics via PHPDoc, PHPStan level 9+ compliance, and Symfony best practices. By Netresearch. ([netresearch/php-modernization-skill](https://github.com/netresearch/php-modernization-skill))
- **php-pro**: Fractal Mastery: Php Pro ([ShasanJain/Master_AG](https://github.com/ShasanJain/Master_AG))
- **php-stack**: Framework-specific conventions for PHP — EventSauce event sourcing, symfony/messenger buses, Doctrine read models ([hpsgd/turtlestack](https://github.com/hpsgd/turtlestack/tree/HEAD/plugins/practices/php-stack))
- **php-structured-edit**: Edit PHP methods, parameters and expressions with named selectors, typed operations and SHA-256 guards. Supports multi-file batches, format-preserving output and optional canonical printing. Review diffs and run lint/project tests; syntax checks do not prove behavior. ([netresearch/php-ast-edit-skill](https://github.com/netresearch/php-ast-edit-skill))
- **php-style**: PHP code conventions centred on object calisthenics: one level of indentation, no else, wrapped primitives, first-class collections, no train wrecks, no abbreviations, small entities, no getters or setters, plus the PHP specifics that support them. ([paulinevos/claude-stuff](https://github.com/paulinevos/claude-stuff/tree/HEAD/plugins/php-style))
